<li>
<p><strong>Identify Key Points and Properties:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>D</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">D</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span></span></span></span> is the foot of the altitude from <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">A</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span></span></span></span> to <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">BC</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0502em;">B</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0715em;">C</span></span></span></span>.</li>
<li><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">E</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span></span></span></span> is the foot of the altitude from <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">B</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0502em;">B</span></span></span></span> to <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">AC</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0715em;">C</span></span></span></span>.</li>
<li><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>F</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">F</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span></span></span></span> is the foot of the altitude from <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">C</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0715em;">C</span></span></span></span> to <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">AB</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0502em;">B</span></span></span></span>.</li>
<li><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>H</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">H</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0813em;">H</span></span></span></span> is the orthocenter of <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">△</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\triangle ABC</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8889em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord">△</span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0502em;">B</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0715em;">C</span></span></span></span>.</li>
<li><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>G</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">G</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">G</span></span></span></span> is the intersection of the line through <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>D</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">D</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span></span></span></span> parallel to <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">AB</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0502em;">B</span></span></span></span> and the line <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>E</mi><mi>F</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">EF</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span></span></span></span>.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Use the Property of the Orthocenter:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>The orthocenter <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>H</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">H</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0813em;">H</span></span></span></span> lies on the altitudes <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>D</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">AD</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span></span></span></span>, <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>B</mi><mi>F</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">BF</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0502em;">B</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span></span></span></span>, and <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>C</mi><mi>E</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">CE</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0715em;">C</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span></span></span></span>.</li>
<li>The points <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>D</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">D</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span></span></span></span>, <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">E</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span></span></span></span>, and <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>F</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">F</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span></span></span></span> are collinear with <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>H</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">H</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0813em;">H</span></span></span></span> on the respective altitudes.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Analyze the Parallel Line:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>Since <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>D</mi><mi>G</mi><mo>∥</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">DG \parallel AB</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span><span class="mord mathnormal">G</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">∥</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0502em;">B</span></span></span></span>, <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>D</mi><mi>G</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>D</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\angle ADG = \angle DAB</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span><span class="mord mathnormal">G</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0502em;">B</span></span></span></span> (corresponding angles).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Consider the Cyclic Quadrilateral:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>The quadrilateral <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>E</mi><mi>H</mi><mi>F</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">AEHF</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0813em;">H</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span></span></span></span> is cyclic because <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>E</mi><mi>H</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>F</mi><mi>H</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>90</mn><mo>∘</mo></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\angle AEH = \angle AFH = 90^\circ</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0813em;">H</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0813em;">H</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6741em;"></span><span class="mord">9</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord">0</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.6741em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mbin mtight">∘</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>.</li>
<li>Therefore, <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>E</mi><mi>H</mi><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>180</mn><mo>∘</mo></msup><mo>−</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>E</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>F</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\angle EHF = 180^\circ - \angle EAF</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0813em;">H</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7574em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord">18</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord">0</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.6741em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mbin mtight">∘</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span></span></span></span>.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Use the Parallel Line Property Again:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>Since <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>D</mi><mi>G</mi><mo>∥</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">DG \parallel AB</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span><span class="mord mathnormal">G</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">∥</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0502em;">B</span></span></span></span>, <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>D</mi><mi>G</mi><mi>E</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>E</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\angle DGE = \angle BAE</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">GE</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0502em;">B</span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span></span></span></span> (alterFnate interior angles).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Relate Angles in Cyclic Quadrilateral:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>In <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">△</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>E</mi><mi>F</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\triangle AEF</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8889em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord">△</span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span></span></span></span>, <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>E</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>E</mi><mi>H</mi><mi>F</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\angle EAF = \angle EHF</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0813em;">H</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span></span></span></span> because they are opposite angles in the cyclic quadrilateral <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>E</mi><mi>H</mi><mi>F</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">AEHF</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0813em;">H</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span></span></span></span>.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Determine <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>C</mi><mi>G</mi><mi>H</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\angle CGH</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0715em;">C</span><span class="mord mathnormal">G</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0813em;">H</span></span></span></span>:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>Since <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>G</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">G</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">G</span></span></span></span> lies on <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>E</mi><mi>F</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">EF</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span></span></span></span> and <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>D</mi><mi>G</mi><mo>∥</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">DG \parallel AB</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span><span class="mord mathnormal">G</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">∥</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0502em;">B</span></span></span></span>, <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>D</mi><mi>G</mi><mi>E</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>E</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\angle DGE = \angle BAE</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">GE</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0502em;">B</span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span></span></span></span>.</li>
<li><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>E</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>E</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>F</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\angle BAE = \angle EAF</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0502em;">B</span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span></span></span></span> (as established).</li>
<li>Therefore, <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>D</mi><mi>G</mi><mi>E</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>E</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>F</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\angle DGE = \angle EAF</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">GE</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span></span></span></span>.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Final Angle Calculation:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>Since <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>E</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>E</mi><mi>H</mi><mi>F</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\angle EAF = \angle EHF</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0813em;">H</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span></span></span></span> and <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>E</mi><mi>H</mi><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>90</mn><mo>∘</mo></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\angle EHF = 90^\circ</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0813em;">H</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span mtight"><span class="mbin mtight">∘</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (as <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math and <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><annotation perpendiculars), we have:
<span class="katex-display"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" display="block"><semantics><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>C</mi><mi>G</mi><mi>H</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>E</mi><mi>H</mi><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>90</mn><mo>∘</mo></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">.</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\angle CGH = \angle EHF = 90^\circ.</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0715em;">C</span><span class="mord mathnormal">G</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0813em;">H</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6922em;"></span><span class="mord">∠</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">E</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0813em;">H</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">F</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7241em;"></span><span class="mord">9</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord">0</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7241em;"><span style="top:-3.113em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mbin mtight">∘</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mord">.</span></span></span></span></span>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<p>Thus, the angle <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi </details>
<h2 id="decontamination">Decontamination</h2>
<p>We conduct decontamination methods on both our pretraining and post-training datasets. Specifically, for pretraining data, we target on math datasets, including GSM8K, MATH, and remove samples that have significant overlaps with the test sets. We use exact match to remove the identical samples and further apply 13-gram deduplication (with a condition that the ratio of longest common sequence should be larger than 0.6) to remove more samples that might cause contamination. For post-training data, we remove more postitive contaminated samples that have overlaps with GSM8K, MATH, Aqua, SAT Math, OlympiadBench, College Math, AIME24, AMC23, etc. with the same filtering method.</p>
<h2 id="summary">Summary</h2>
<p>This time, we're releasing a new model series focused on mathematical capabilities, zen-Math, built upon the zen foundation. Our flagship model, zen-Math-72B-Instruct, outperforms proprietary models such as GPT-4o and Claude 3.5 in math-related tasks. Given the current limitation of English-only support, we plan to release bilingual models that support both English and Chinese shortly, with the development of multilingual models also in the pipeline.
